# Agents · T · part 13

1,075 entries, test-quality-reviewer to test-runner. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **test-quality-reviewer**: Reviews test code for quality problems — tautologies, implementation coupling, missing edge cases, flaky patterns, weak assertions. Use after tests pass to evaluate whether they actually catch regressions. Spawn on individual test files or directories. ([kookr-ai/kookr](https://github.com/kookr-ai/kookr/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Use this agent after you have written or modified tests and need them reviewed for correctness, meaningful assertions, mock validity and clean test code structure. ([noahlz/claude-plugins](https://github.com/noahlz/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-workflow/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Reviews test files for assertion quality. Flags tests that only verify structure (not NULL, count &gt; 0) without asserting specific expected business outcomes. Use after implementation to audit test quality before PR. ([alexander-vyh/escapement](https://github.com/alexander-vyh/escapement/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md) · ★3 · 3 copies)
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Returns findings on the test files handed to it, covering tests that would keep passing if the behaviour they name were broken, weighted toward over-mocking. A step of the typescript-code-and-test-standards skill, run when that skill's bundled procedures section calls for it. ([AlexJSully/AlexJSully-Portfolio](https://github.com/AlexJSully/AlexJSully-Portfolio/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/typescript-code-and-test-standards/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Orchestrate comprehensive mutation testing workflow for test quality analysis using semantic code mutations and parallel test execution ([citadelgrad/scott-cc](https://github.com/citadelgrad/scott-cc/blob/HEAD/plugins/mutation-testing/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Reviews test code changes against the testing strategy -- fixes placement, removes redundancy, improves quality. Use after implementation and before PR creation, or standalone on any branch/PR. ([Alterna-Dev-Studio/dev-env-template](https://github.com/Alterna-Dev-Studio/dev-env-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Audits spec files for quality issues: skipped tests, missing assertions, implementation-detail testing, duplicate test names. ([FabienDehopre/triple-yahtzee-score-board](https://github.com/FabienDehopre/triple-yahtzee-score-board/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Use this agent to judge whether the tests a change adds or modifies can fail for the right reason, covering behavior-versus-implementation coupling, flakiness risk, and coverage gaps. Invoke it when a change adds or modifies test files, or when a test is flaky. ([limn-works/scp](https://github.com/limn-works/scp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Reviews test files for quality anti-patterns. Looks for tautological assertions, mock-shape mirroring, assertion erosion across commits, skipped/focused tests, and over-mocking. Invoked by /review and ship-pr. ([marcanthonyrosa/saucestack](https://github.com/marcanthonyrosa/saucestack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Dedicated test quality reviewer for PR diffs. Checks (T1–T9): behavior-over-implementation, mock fidelity, edge case coverage, missing tests for new logic, test naming clarity, zero-assertion/mock-call-only/not.toThrow() detection (T6 Hard Rule), boundary operator coverage, stale mock contracts, and test isolation. Spawned conditionally in review Phase 2 when test files or new exported functions without spec changes are detected. Also usable standalone after any test-writing session. ([wasikarn/devflow](https://github.com/wasikarn/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Reviews test code for coverage gaps, behavior-focused testing, flakiness risk, edge case coverage, and test structure quality ([Brite-Nites/brite-claude-plugins](https://github.com/Brite-Nites/brite-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/workflows/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md))
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Opus-powered test quality reviewer. Verifies tests are meaningful — not just annotated. Checks real behavior is tested (not mocks), spec test cases are covered, assertions (including property-based/fuzz test invariants) would catch real bugs, and edge cases from the spec are exercised. Also projects future impact: test brittleness, coverage gaps that grow dangerous as the feature evolves. Use alongside pr-reviewer before merge. ([CagesThrottleUs/private-ai-harness](https://github.com/CagesThrottleUs/private-ai-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md))
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Use when reviewing a git diff for test quality in the vending-machine repo — wrong test level, implementation-coupled tests, weak assertions, mocked value objects, missing edge cases, or gaps against the challenge's executable examples. Invoke proactively when a diff adds or changes tests, or when the user asks "¿están bien estos tests?", "revisa los tests", "is this well tested". ([colateguate/icar-vending-machine](https://github.com/colateguate/icar-vending-machine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md))
- **test-quality-reviewer**: 테스트를 추가·수정했을 때 TDD 준수, 픽스처 도달가능성, 단언 강도, 커버리지의 실효성을 검토한다. 통과하는 테스트가 버그를 덮고 있는지를 본다. ([coupon-yaho/cy-waiting](https://github.com/coupon-yaho/cy-waiting/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md))
- **test-quality-reviewer**: テストコード品質の専門レビューエージェント。古典学派（Classical School）のテスト原則に基づいてテストコードを検証する。モック制限（次元10）・振る舞いテスト（次元11）・テスト価値（次元12）・テストケース網羅性（次元13）の4次元でレビューする。ルール自体が普遍的原則であり、spec文書なしでも動作する。spec文書が提供された場合は参考にする。spec-based-code-review スキルおよび test-review スキルのオーケストレーターから委譲されて動作する。 ([DIO0550/d-market-spec](https://github.com/DIO0550/d-market-spec/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-based-code-review-plugin/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md))
- **test-quality-reviewer**: ../../packs/ts-next/reviewers/test-quality-reviewer.md ([dylanpulver/code-factory](https://github.com/dylanpulver/code-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md))
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Use when a verified change to production logic or tests is ready for delivery, or dispatch directly to audit existing tests or propose a test strategy. For a change review, dispatch with five labelled lines and nothing else, Worktree, Base or range, Revision, Accepted scope (a file path, or the ask quoted when no file holds it) and Scope folder; for an audit, the question and its target. Its rubric sets mutation scope, waits and budgets. Loads its rubric from the test-quality-review skill. ([giostriquer/workshop](https://github.com/giostriquer/workshop/blob/HEAD/plugins/workbench/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md))
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Reviews the tests in a Renovo diff — whether changed code is covered to the 80% changed-line / 85% total floor, and whether the tests actually assert the behaviour that changed, including permission and isolation tests, both database engines and edge cases. Read-only. Use on any branch or PR that changes src/ or tests/, or when asked to "review the tests". ([gwpreston16/Renovo](https://github.com/gwpreston16/Renovo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md))
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Reviews the tests of a Flightdeck branch for tests that cannot fail: a weak or removed assertion, a red by error instead of assertion, a test that passes on the stub, a mock-only check, a hidden wait, an implementation-detail test, a mock of a managed dependency, leaked domain knowledge. Read-only. Use before a hand-off or when accepting a branch; give the worktree and, if not main, the base. ([jnerytech/flightdeck](https://github.com/jnerytech/flightdeck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md))
- **test-quality-reviewer**: Reviews changed test files for false-green defects — assertions that prove nothing, tautological checks, and mocks that void the test. Standalone or dispatched by the review include. ([Jodre11/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/Jodre11/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-review-suite/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md))

- **test-quality-reviewer**: Review tests for meaningfulness. Rejects tautology tests, mock-only tests, framework tests, sleep-driven tests, and other forms that always pass regardless of what the code does. Spawned in parallel with code-reviewer during /implement Stage 2 whenever a test file is in the diff, or when logic was added in one of the four invariant areas without a paired test. ([robertanton81/dobot-public](https://github.com/robertanton81/dobot-public/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-quality-reviewer.md))
- **test-quality-reviewer**: MANDATORY adversarial audit of the tests in a diff — runs in every review, before anything ships. Reverts each mechanism and re-runs to show which tests actually bite.
